# 十、监事会报告

## 1、2014年监事会工作情况

2014年，本公司监事会秉承“履行职责、防控风险、促进发展”的工作方针，严格按照《商业银行公司治理指引》的要求，认真、勤勉、审慎地行使股东大会所赋予的职责，充分组织好、发挥好内审部门审计检查职能作用，采取日常观察与参加会议以及组织撰写评价报告等方式对全行重点经营管理活动进行了检查监督。

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="imgs/img_in_image_box_462_290_1077_652.jpg" alt="Image" width="51%" /></div>


一是全面强化风险管控工作的监督。根据本公司信用风险、操作风险和声誉风险管理情况，按照中国银监会办公厅《关于建立健全“双线”风险防控责任制的通知》要求，联合董事会制定印发了《关于建立健全青海银行“双线”风险防控主体责任的通知》，全面落实本公司“双线”风险防控责任制，进一步提高全行风险防控水平。同时，紧紧围绕2014年全国银行业监督管理工作会议提出的七类风险防控要求，不断强化风险管理工作，持续关注政府平台、房地产、高载能企业贷款风险及风险抵补准备；加强案件防控工作的监督，密切关注负面舆情影响与化解工作，支持促进全行安全稳健发展。



二是加强财务管理工作的检查。密切关注和监督影响财务收益的各项指标的执行情况，重点监督检查经营层对董事会核批的财务预算执行情况；监督检查各分支机构对总行下达财务收支计划执行情况及核拨费用的使用与管理情况；监督检查账务核算与年度利润的真实性；对重大项目支出实施专项监督检查，提出改进提升财务管理的意见和建议。

三是加强高管履职行为的考察。充分发挥监事会“观察员”和“监督员”作用，重点监督董事在决策和风险管控方面的履职行为，监督经营高管层对董事会决策的执行情况，监督经营高管层在推进业务转型和精细化管理执行力方面的履职行为，以及对风险管控的敏感度和处置情况。不断完善对董事、高管人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度对董事会和高管的履职情况进行考察和监督。

四是全面强化内部审计工作。2014年，监事会会同董事会组织并指导内部审计部门对全行所属各单位经营管理活动开展了39项常规审计和专项审计。进一步拓宽审计领域，消除审计空白点，实现对全行各业务条线、全业务流程的审计。重点加大对新业务、业务管理薄弱环节和屡查屡犯问题的审计和后续审计。建立健全违规问责机制，增强审计工作的严肃性和强制性。建设非现场审计系统，提高审计工作的信息化、科学化水平。全年审计发现问题共计1282条，其中，已整改1201条，未整改81条。进一步加大了审计问题通报、问责和整改监督力度，密切跟踪开展后续整改审计检查，本年度审计检查发现问题综合整改率达到93.68%，在一定程度上提高了审计检查的有效性，提高了整改率，内部审计的风险控制和案件管控作用得到显著提升。

五是持续推进监事会自身建设。继续将监事会工作融入日常经营管理活动当中，充分利用和发挥内审部门、外部机构和银监局的监督检查成果，推进持续跟踪整改落实。进一步完善工作评价报告制度，密切加强与本公司董事会、经营管理层及监管部门的工作沟通与协调，全力支持和配合董事会、经营层的工作，共同促进本公司持续健康发展。报告期内，监事会以现场和通讯方式共组织召开了9次监事会会议，审议了涉及年度报告、财务预决算方案、利润分配方案以及监事会相关工作报告等21项议案。此期间，各位监事认真履行职责，对每一项重要议题都进行了认真审核，并充分发表了独立的意见和建议。

## 十、监事会报告

## 2、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

报告期内，根据本公司章程及中国银监会相关要求，本公司监事会通过与董事、高管人员访谈并列席董事会议、高管层经营会议及日常工作观察，按照有关股份制商业银行董事、高管人员履职尽职要求，对董事、高管人员2014年度履职情况作出如下评价意见：

(1)监事会认为：2014年，董事会准确研判内外形势，正确把握发展方向，科学制定战略决策，引领和推动了全行业务经营和改革开放。全行上下改革开放意识不断增强，步伐明显加快，效果持续显现，在机构建设、社区银行、经营转型、同业合作、对外开放等领域不断迈出新步伐，公司治理更加完善，发展基础更加坚实。董事会成员能够以维护股东权益和推进本公司稳健发展为目标，诚实守信、勤勉尽职、合规履职，以负责、审慎、科学的态度对年度内提交的涉及到公司机构发展、资本管理以及人力资源等多方面问题进行了深度思考，对资本融资、设立分支机构、聘任高级管理人员等多项决议充分发表合适的、独立的、积极有效的意见，充分行使了本公司章程所赋予的职权，充分发挥了作为董事会成员的决策作用，有利地维护了全体股东的各项权益，对本公司的发展战略决策和风险管理较好地履行了董事应尽之责。

(2)监事会认为：2014年，本公司高管团队成员克服形势多变、任务繁重的困难，深入分析新常态下面临的新形势，积极推动精细化管理和业务转型，在困境中取得了出色成绩，综合管理与业务创新的技能和经验得到了不断提升和积累，促进全行取得了业务持续攀升、管理能力增强、经营效益再创新高的良好业绩。特别是原行长钟园先生离任后，经董事会授权由王丽董事长代行行长职责期间，保证了经营管理工作的连续性，各项工作开展平稳、有序、有效。

## 3、2015年监事会工作重点

2015年，本公司监事会将严格按照《商业银行公司治理指引》及《青海银行章程》等相关规定，持续加强自身建设，不断完善监督管理手段，有效推进各项监督检查，把监事会监督工作与全行日常经营管理活动紧密结合起来，推动本公司更好地实现稳健经营和可持续发展的目标。

(1)加强对风险管控工作的监督。密切关注并监督关乎本公司发展的风险控制状况，绷紧风险管控这根弦，强化逆经济周期的风险意识，特别是在当前更加严峻的经济金融形势下，监督董事会和经营层在重大问题上风险防控的政策、程序和措施的落实执行情况。重点监督经营层在整个经营过程中风险防控措施的运用情况。进一步强化对案件防控工作的监督，促进全行安全稳健发展。加强对财务管理行为的监督，重点监督检查经营层对董事会核批的财务预算执行情况，各分支机构对总行下达财务收支计划执行情况及核拨费用的使用与管理情况及年度利润的真实性，加强对重大项目支出的监督检查。

(2)加强对董事、监事及高管履职情况的考核和监督。进一步改进对董事、经营层高管人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度观察、监督、评价履职情况，提出监督评价意见和工作改进建议。加强与董事、高管人员的充分沟通，全力支持董事会和经营层开展各项工作，积极为全行工作建言献策，共同推动本公司经营管理水平再上新台阶。

(3)加强对重大、重要政策实施落实情况的监督。对董事会制定的发展战略和风险控制策略的科学性、合理性和有效性进行评估，围绕新常态下经济社会发展的外部形势、金融领域改革的总体步调和本公司改革发展的内在条件，重点关注新一轮增资扩股方案的确定和实施工作。关注经营管理工作，推进业务转型和精细化管理，重点考察中小微金融业务、个人金融业务转型政策的落实情况，绩效管理政策的落实及其效应情况以及信息科技治理、安全运行、维护管理情况。

(4)持续加强监事会自身建设。找准新常态下监事会工作的落脚点，根据监管部门对公司治理架构的新要求，进一步完善监事会组织架构，明确监事会职责权限，建立健全各项监督管理制度，对监事履职情况进行考核评价。在有效组织执行审计检查、列席会议、日常观察等监督手段的基础，进一步创新监督管理手段，探索建立和实施监事会调研巡查与质询工作机制，在组织开展现场审计工作的同时，组织监事会同步开展专项和综合性调研活动以及必要的质询工作。加强与监管部门的沟通联系，认真听取监管意见，持续强化自身建设。

# X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

## 1. Work of the Board of Supervisors for 2014

In the year 2014, the Company's Board of Supervisors exercised the guideline of “exercise duties, prevent and control risks, and promote development, strictly in accordance with the Instructions for Governance of Commercial Bank Company, perform duties granted by the shareholders’ assembly carefully, diligently and prudently, and check and inspection daily management activities in forms of observing and participating conference and organizing assessment reports.

Firstly, intensify supervision of risk management and control. According to the Company's credit risks, operation risk and reputation risks, as required by the Notice on Establishment of "Dual Lines" Risk Prevention and Control issued by the Office Department of China Banking Regulatory Committee, cooperated with the Board of Directors to draft up and print the Notice on Establishment and Perfection of "Dual Lines" Risk Prevention and Control Notice by the Bank of Qinghai as Principal to implement the Company's "dual lines" risk prevention and control responsibility and enhance the bank's risk prevention and control proficiency. Meanwhile, according to risk prevention and control requirements of seven categories by the State Banking Industry Supervision Conference 2014, intensify risk management, continued to focus on enterprise loan risks and risk make-up provision related to government platform, real estate, high energy-loading enterprise loan risks; reinforced supervision over prevention and control of cases, paid close attention to influences and solving of public comment, and promoted secure, stable and healthy development of the Bank.

Secondly, reinforce inspection of financial management. Closely focus on and supervise execution of indicators affecting financial yields, and supervise execution of financial budget by the operation tier approved by the Board of Directors; supervise and check execution of income and expenditure of the financial program required by the head office, and use and management of appropriated funds; supervise and inspect authenticity of computation and annual profit; carry out special supervision and inspection for expenses of key projects, and propose opinions and suggestions for improving financial management.

Thirdly, reinforce investigation over performance by senior management. Fully play functions of the Board of Supervisors as “observer” and “supervisor”, supervise performance of directors in decision making and risk management and control, supervise execution of decisions by senior management in charge of operation made by the Board of Directors, supervise senior management’s in boosting business transformation and refining management and sensitivity and handling of risk prevention and control. Continuously perfect assessment report system on performance by the Directors and Senior Management, and investigate and supervise performance of the Board of Directors and senior management in all-around views.

Fourthly, reinforce internal audit. In the year 2014, the Board of Supervisors and the Board of Directors organized and guided internal auditing departments to carry out 39 regular audits and special audits for operation and management activities of the Bank. Further expand audit fields, eliminate audit blank spots and carry out audit for all lines and business processes of the Bank. Focus on audit and successive audit for new business, weak business links and re-incurring issues. Establish accountability system for any breaching, and enhance seriousness and coerciveness of audit. Build non-site audit system, and improve information and scientific proficiency of audit. Throughout the year, 1282 issues were discovered, 1201 issues were corrected and 81 issues were not corrected. Further increase strength of announcement of audit issues, accountability and correction supervision, and closely track audit check in subsequent correction. Overall correction rate of issues discovered in audit this year reaches 93.68%, which improves effectiveness of audit and increases correction rate, and risk control and case management and control of internal audit have been improved.

# X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

Fifthly, continue to advance construction of the Board of Supervisors. Continue to combine daily work of the Board of Supervisors into daily operation and management, fully utilize and play supervision and check of internal audit department, external bodies and the Banking Regulatory Committee and continue to follow up and implement. Further perfect report system on work assessment, closely reinforce communication and coordination with the Company's Board of Directors, Operation and Management Tier and supervision authorities, fully support and cooperate with work by the Board of Directors and the operation tier and promote sustainable and healthy development of the Company. During the reporting period, the Board of Supervisors convoked 9 meetings of the Board of Supervisors in field and telecommunication, and reviewed 21 issues involving annual report, financial budget and final cost plans, profit distribution plans and work report by the Board of Supervisors. During the reporting period, the supervisors exercised duties, reviewed and audited each key issues, and expressed independent opinions and suggestions.

## 2. Independent opinions delivered by the Board of Supervisors for related issues

During the reporting period, according to requirements set out in the Articles of Association and China Banking Regulatory Committee, the Board of Supervisors of the Company interviewed directors and senior managers, attended operation and management conference and observed daily work, according to due diligence requirements for directors and senior managers by stock commercial banks, and delivered assessment opinions for performance by directors and senior managers in 2014:

(1) The Board of Supervisors thought that: in the year 2014, the Board of Directors researched and judged domestic and overseas situations accurately, drafted up strategy decisions scientifically, and led and boosts business operation, opening up and reform in the Bank. Enhanced all the staff's awareness of opening up and reform, accelerated step towards organization construction, community bank, business transformation, inter-bank cooperation and open up with continuous effects, corporate governance was perfect and development foundation is solid. Members of the Board of Directors aimed to maintain shareholders' equity and boost the Company's stable and healthy development, acted in faith, due diligence and in conformity with requirements, provide profound reflections on company development, capital management HR mentioned in the annual report in a responsible, prudent and scientific manner, delivered proper, independent, active and effective opinions over capital financing, branch establishment and senior manager engagement, exercised duties endowed by Articles of Association of the Company, fully played the decision-making role as members of the Board of Directors, maintained equities of all shareholders and exercised duties for the Company's development strategy decision-making and risk management.

(2) The Board of Supervisor thought that: in the year 2014, the Company's senior management overcame difficulties like varying situations and heavy tasks, analyzed new situations under China's new normal, actively boosted refining management and business transformation, achieved outstanding performance in difficulties, and skill and experiment of comprehensive management and business innovation to realize outstanding performance with increased trade volume, powerful management and more business profits. In particular, after the former president Mr. Zhong Yuan resigned, as authorized by the Board of Directors, the President Wang Li acted as the President to ensure continuity of operation and management, and the work carried out stably, orderly and effectively.

## 3. Key work of the Board of Supervisors in 2015

In the year 2015, in accordance with the Instructions to Governance of Commercial Bank Company and the Articles of Association of the Bank of Qinghai, the Board of Supervisors of the Company reinforces its own construction, perfects supervision and management means, effectively advances supervision and checks, combines work of the Board of Supervisors and daily business and operation, and boosts the Company's realizing stable and healthy operation and sustainable development.

# X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

(1) Reinforce supervision over risk management and control. Closely focus on and supervision risk control related to the Company's development, keep a close eye on risk management and control, intensify risk awareness of reverse economic cycle, in particular, under the more serious economic and financial situations, and supervise implementation of policies, procedures and measures of risk prevention and control by the Board of Directors and the Operation Tier. Supervise implementation of risk control measures by the Operation Tier during the entire operation course. Further reinforce supervision over prevention and control, and promote secure, stable and healthy development of the Bank. Reinforce supervision over financial management, supervise and inspection execution of financial budget approved by the Board of Directors, execution of financial income and expenses by branches delivered by the head office, use and management of verified and appropriated funds and authenticity of annual profit, and intensify supervision and inspection over expenses for key projects.

(2) Reinforce assessment and supervision for directors, supervisors and senior managers' performance. Further improve assessment and reporting system for performance of directors and senior managers of the Operation Tier, and propose supervision and assessment opinions and improvement suggestions. Reinforce communication with directors and senior management, actively provide suggestions for the Bank, and advance the Company's operation and management to a new level.

(3) Reinforce supervision for implantation of key policies. Scientific nature, reasonability and effectiveness of development strategy and risk control strategy determined by the Board of Directors are assessed, based on external situations for economic and social development under new normal, overall progress for reform in financial field and internal conditions for reform and development of the Company, and determination and implementation for a new round of capital increasing and share expansion are centered. Focus on operation and management, advance business transformation and refining management, inspect implementation of medium and micro-financial business, implementation of personal financial business transformation policy, implementation of performance management policy and its effects, and information science& technology governance, secure operation and maintenance.

(4) Continue to reinforce construction of the Board of Supervisor. Find out a foothold for work by the Board of Supervisors under new normal, according to new requirements on corporate governance structure by the supervision authorities, perfect organization structure of the Board of Supervisors, clarify duties and authorities of the Board of Supervisors, establish and perfect supervision management system, and assess performance of supervisors. While effectively organizing audit check, attending conference and observing on a daily basis, further innovate with supervision management means, explore to establish and implement the investigation and research tour inspection and inquiry mechanism. When the field audit is in progress, organize the Board of Supervisors to conduct special and comprehensive investigation and research activities and necessary inquiry. Reinforce communication with the supervision authorities, take supervision opinions and continue to reinforce its own buildup.

## 十二、财务报告

本公司2014年度财务报告经青海大正会计师事务所有限公司注册会计师吴海泉、陈宝萍根据中国注册会计师独立审计准则，实施了审计工作，审计报告和经审计确认后的财务会计报表如下：

#### 审计报告

青海银行股份有限公司全体股东：

大正会师审字(2015)第247号

我们审计了后附的青海银行股份有限公司(以下简称贵公司)财务报表，包括2014年12月31日的资产负债表，2014年度的利润表、现金流量表和股东权益变动表以及财务报表附注。

## 一、管理层对财务报表的责任

编制和公允列报财务报表是贵公司管理层的责任。这种责任包括：(1)按照企业会计准则的规定编制财务报表，并使其实现公允反映；(2)设计、执行和维护必要的内部控制，以使财务报表不存在由于舞弊或错误而导致的重大错报。

## 二、注册会计师责任

我们的责任是在执行审计工作的基础上对财务报表发表审计意见。我们按照中国注册会计师审计准则的规定计划执行了审计工作。中国注册会计师审计准则要求我们遵守中国注册会计师职业道德守则，计划和执行审计工作以对财务报表是否不存在重大错报获取合理保证。

审计工作涉及实施审计程序，以获取有关财务报表金额和披露的审计证据。选择的审计程序取决于注册会计师的判断，包括对由于舞弊或错误导致的财务报表重大错报风险的评估。在进行风险评估时，注册会计师考虑与财务报表编制和公允列报相关的内部控制，以设计恰当的审计程序，但目的并非对内部控制的有效性发表意见。审计工作还包括评价管理层选用会计政策的恰当性和作出会计估计的合理性，以及评价财务报表的总体列报。

我们相信，我们获取的审计证据是充分、适当的，为发表审计意见提供了基础。

## 三、审计意见

我们认为，青海银行股份有限公司财务报表在所有重大方面按照企业会计准则的规定编制，公允反映了贵公司2014年12月31日的财务状况以及2014年度的经营成果和现金流量。

## XII. Financial report

The Company's financial report of 2014 was audited by CPA of China Wu Haiquan and Chen Baoping engaged by Qinghai Dazheng Certified Public Accounts Co., Ltd. and the audit report and financial statement after audit and confirmation are given below:

### Audit Report

D.Z.K.S.S.Z. (2015) No.247

#### To all shareholders of Bank of Qinghai Co., Ltd :

We have audited the attached financial report forms of Bank of Qinghai Co., Ltd. (hereafter referred to as the Company), including balance sheet on December 31, 2014, profit sheet, statement of change in stockholder equity, cash flow sheet and annotation for financial reporting for 2014.

### I. Responsibility for accounting reporting forms by the management

Preparation and fair disclosure of financial statement are your Company management's responsibilities, which include (1) prepare financial statements in accordance with the corporate accounting code to be reflected fairly; (2) design, execute and maintain necessary internal control to make the financial statement free of any significant errors due to embezzlement or errors.

### II. CPA's responsibility

Our responsibility is to make comment based on auditing the reporting form. We have performed according to auditing principle for China's CPA, which requires us to observe vocational and moral standard, plan and perform audit and without key mistake.

Audit involves audit proof and audited amount and disclosed audit proof. The selected audit procedure depends on CPA's judge, including assessment on possible key mistake by embezzlement or mistake. When assessing risk, CPA will consider relevant internal control related to compilation of the reporting form and appropriate audit procedure, but not to make comment on validity of internal control. Audit also includes correction of accounting policy and reasonability of accounting estimate and comment on the whole reporting forms.

We believe that the audit proof obtained by us is sufficient and reasonable and paves the way for making audit comment.

### III. Audit comment

We think, the financial statements of the Bank of Qinghai in all significant aspects in accordance with the Accounting Standards for Enterprise has been compiling the provisions, fairly reflects financial situations of your company dated on December 31, 2014, business achievement and cash flow of the year 2014.

Qinghai Dizheng Certified Public Accountants Co., Ltd
